S16边界：性情、相貌、疾病、女命、小儿、六亲、孕生男女及古代富贵贫贱词均只生成专题候选。疾病条文固定零医学诊断贡献；性别与婚育条文保留历史语境，不转为现代规范；六亲逐人物建身份，命主结构不得单独决定他人行为。所有古代结果词必须交S17建立状态、动作与正式终点链。



### SMTH结果词角色规则

```text
SMTH_RESULT_ROLE_RULE_ENUM=R01_PROPER_NAME_SUBSTRING|R02_TECHNICAL_SUFFIX|R03_TECHNICAL_PREFIX|R04_TECHNICAL_LOCATION|R05_ENTITY_OR_RESOURCE_INPUT|R06_OFFICE_OBJECT|R07_CASE_EXAMPLE|R08_METHOD_EXAMPLE|R09_EXPLICIT_ANALOGY_SCOPE|R11_FOUNDATION_GENERAL_NATURE|R12_TRANSFORMATION_INPUT|R13_META_CONCEPT_PAIR|R15_META_DISCUSSION|R16_DIRECT_NEGATION|R17_POST_TERM_LIMITATION|R18_ASSERTION_LIMIT|R19_SOURCE_RESULT_CANDIDATE
SMTH_RESULT_ROLE_RULE_COUNTS=R01_PROPER_NAME_SUBSTRING:2|R02_TECHNICAL_SUFFIX:548|R03_TECHNICAL_PREFIX:135|R04_TECHNICAL_LOCATION:7|R05_ENTITY_OR_RESOURCE_INPUT:116|R06_OFFICE_OBJECT:22|R07_CASE_EXAMPLE:1357|R09_EXPLICIT_ANALOGY_SCOPE:17|R11_FOUNDATION_GENERAL_NATURE:88|R12_TRANSFORMATION_INPUT:18|R13_META_CONCEPT_PAIR:123|R15_META_DISCUSSION:231|R16_DIRECT_NEGATION:475|R17_POST_TERM_LIMITATION:23|R18_ASSERTION_LIMIT:5|R19_SOURCE_RESULT_CANDIDATE:4963
SMTH_RESULT_ROLE_CLASSIFICATION_PRIORITY=FALSE_POSITIVE>TECHNICAL>ENTITY_OR_RESOURCE>PROFESSION_OBJECT>CASE>METHOD_OR_ANALOGY>GENERAL_NATURE>TRANSFORMATION_INPUT>META>NEGATION_OR_LIMITATION>TRUE_CANDIDATE
SMTH_RESULT_ROLE_CLASSIFICATION_UNIT=EACH_EXACT_TERM_OCCURRENCE_WITH_CLAUSE_AND_SEGMENT_PARENT
```

同一词在不同上下文中分别登记。人名子串、技术名词、职业对象、关系／资源输入、命例事实、方法类比和一般性质均不得因出现结果词而进入现实终点；只有`TRUE_CANDIDATE`与`NEGATED_OR_LIMITED_CANDIDATE`可作为仍待成立门审查的结果候选，且二者仍无直接终点权限。
